<script>
	var user_partner = '';
	var public_guest = '';
	var public_transparent = '';
	var public_widgetid = '';
	var public_guest_parameter = ((public_guest == 'yes') ? '&guest=yes' : '') + ((public_transparent == 'yes') ? '&transparent=yes' : '') + ((public_widgetid > 0) ? '&widgetid=' + public_widgetid : '');
	var public_iframe_parameter = '';
	var framework_type = '';
</script>



	
	<script>
	if (top==self && top.location.href.match(/iframe/)) top.location.href = top.location.href.replace(/iframe=yes/, '');
	if (top!=self && !self.location.href.match(/modal=true/) && !self.location.href.match(/KeepThis/) && !self.location.href.match(/box/) && framework_type != 'popup') {

		/* IFRAME */

		var parent_url = '#' + encodeURIComponent(decodeURIComponent(document.location.hash.replace( /^#/, '' )));
		var public_hash = 'WwkhCP';
		public_iframe_parameter = '&iframe=yes';

		// check iframe
		if (!self.location.href.match(/iframe=yes/)) {
			var value = self.location.href;
			if (value.match(/\?/)) value = value.replace(/\?/, '?iframe=yes' + public_guest_parameter + '&');
			else if (value.match(/\#/)) value = value.replace(/#/, '?iframe=yes' + public_guest_parameter + "#");
			else value = value + '?iframe=yes' + public_guest_parameter;
			self.location.href = value;
		}

		function iframe_init() {

			iframe_height();
			iframe_links();

			$('#logo').attr('href', server_url + '/partner?iframe=yes');
		}

		function iframe_height() {

			if (parent_url != '') {
				var h = $('body').outerHeight();
				h = Math.round(h);
				var p_url = decodeURIComponent(decodeURIComponent(parent_url.replace( /^#/, '' )));
				if (p_url == '') return false;

				window.parent.postMessage('h='+h + '&id=msp_full_WwkhCP-&hash=false&p=1', p_url);

				setTimeout("iframe_height()", 500);
			}
		}

		function iframe_links() {

			$('*[onclick], *[onchange], *[ondblclick], form[action], a[href]').each(function() {

				var attribute = 'onclick';
				if ($(this).is('[onchange]')) attribute = 'onchange';
				else if ($(this).is('[ondblclick]')) attribute = 'ondblclick';
				else if ($(this).is('[action]')) attribute = 'action';
				else if ($(this).is('[href]')) attribute = 'href';
				
				if ($(this).hasClass('header_planlink')) return true;

				var value = $(this).attr(attribute);
				if (typeof(value) != "undefined" && value.match(server_url) && !value.match('iframe') && $(this).attr('target') != '_blank') {
				
					if (value.match(/\?/)) value = value.replace(/\?/, '?iframe=yes' + public_guest_parameter + '&');
					else if (value.match(/\'$/)) value = value.replace(/\'$/, '?iframe=yes' + public_guest_parameter + "'");
					else if (value.match(/\#/)) value = value.replace(/#/, '?iframe=yes' + public_guest_parameter + "#" );
					else value = value + '?iframe=yes' + public_guest_parameter;

					if (attribute == 'href') {
						$(this).attr(attribute, value + parent_url);
					} else {
						$(this).attr(attribute, value);
					}
				}
				if (public_guest == 'yes' && attribute == 'href' && !value.match('WwkhCP')) {
					$(this).attr('target', '_blank');
				}
			});
		}

		function iframe_cookie(name) {

		    if (typeof(name) == "undefined") return false;
		    name = name + '=';

		    var carray = document.cookie.split(';');
		    for (var i=0; i < carray.length; i++) {
		        var c = carray[i];
		        while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1,c.length);
		        if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) return c.substring(name.length,c.length);
			}
		    return false;
		}

		$(document).ready(function() {
			iframe_init();
		});

	}
	</script>

<script>
	function cookieConsent(message, button, domain) {
		if (getCookie("_msp_cc") == "yes") {
			cookieConsentInit();
			return false;
		}
		$("body").append('<div id="ccbox"><div id="ccbox-inner"><div class="ck-logo"><div></div></div><div class="ck-content">' + message + '<br /><a style="white-space: nowrap;" href="https://www.meinspielplan.de/privacy.html">' + button + '</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<a class="ck-imprint" href="https://www.meinspielplan.de/imprint.html">Impressum</a><br /><div id="ccbox-button" onclick="cookieConsentClose(\''+domain+'\'); return false;">✓ Akzeptieren</div><br /><span id="ccbox-deny" onclick="cookieConsentDeny(\''+domain+'\'); return false;">Nur notwendige Cookies</span><br /></div></div></div>').addClass('ck');
	}
	function cookieConsentDeny(domain) {
		cookie_gaOptout();
		cookieConsentClose(domain);
	}
	function cookieConsentClose(domain) {
		if (domain != '') domain = "." + domain;
		setCookie("_msp_cc", "yes", 31536E3, domain); $("#ccbox").hide();
		cookieConsentInit();
	};
	function getCookie(a) {
		for (var b=document.cookie.split(";"),c=0;c<b.length;c++) { var e=b[c].split("="); if (e[0].indexOf(a)>=0) return decodeURIComponent(e[1]) }
		return false
	}
	function setCookie(a,b,c,e) {
		if (c == false) document.cookie=a+"="+encodeURIComponent(b)+';path=/;domain='+e+";"
		else {
			var d=new Date; d.setTime(d.getTime()+c*1E3); document.cookie=a+"="+encodeURIComponent(b)+';expires="'+d.toUTCString()+'";path=/;domain='+e+";"
		}
	}

	function cookieConsentInit() {
		$('body').removeClass('ck');
		lazyload_refresh();
		if (typeof(ga_init) == 'function') ga_init();
	}

	function cookie_gaOptout() {
		document.cookie = 'ga-disable=true; expires=Thu, 31 Dec 2099 23:59:59 UTC; path=/';
		window['ga-disable'] = true;
	}
</script>

		function language_cookie(lang) {
			setCookie("_msp_l", lang, false, 'meinspielplan.de');
		}
		function language_change(lang) {
			if (lang == language) tb_remove();
			else {
				language_cookie(lang);
				window.location.href = languages_url[lang];
			}
		}
		function language_change_open() {

			$('.ui-tooltip').remove();

		}

<script>
var faq_keywords = false;

function header_faq_open() {

	if (!faq_keywords) {
		$.post(server_url + "/faq.html?a=keywords", function(keywords) {
			faq_keywords = [];
			for (const i in keywords) {
				faq_keywords.push(keywords[i]);
			}
		}, 'json');
	}

	if (!$('#header_faq').hasClass('header_faq_preprend')) {
		$('.global_top').prepend($('#header_faq'));
		$('#header_faq').addClass('header_faq_preprend');
	}
	$('body').addClass('header_faq_show');
	$('#faq_search').val('').focus();

	if ($('#header_faq_overlay').length == 0) {
		$('body').append('<div id="header_faq_overlay" onclick="header_faq_close();"></div>');
	}

	$('#header_faq_overlay').show();

	faq_scroll_start = $(window).scrollTop();
}

function header_faq_close() {

	$('#faq_search').blur();
	$('body').removeClass('header_faq_show').removeClass('header_faq_bottom');
	$('#faq_search').val('');
	$('.faq').removeClass('faq_open').hide();
	$('.faq_resultcount').removeClass('faq_resultcount_show').html('');
	$('.faq_results').removeClass('faq_results_found');
	$('#header_faq_overlay').hide();
	$('.faq_results').css('height', 'auto');
	$('body').css('overflow', 'auto');
}

function header_faq_focus() {
	$('#faq_search').focus();
}

var input_stopped = 0;
var input_delay = 1000;
function faq_check_pre() {

	if (!faq_keywords) {
		setTimeout("faq_check_pre();", input_delay);
	} else {
		input_stopped = Date.now();
		setTimeout("faq_check();", input_delay);
	}
}

function faq_check() {

	var t = Date.now();
	var diff = t - input_stopped;
	if (diff >= input_delay) faq_check_fire();
}

function faq_check_fire() {

	input_stopped= 0;

	var text = $('#faq_search').val().toLowerCase();

	$('.faq').hide();
	if (text == '') $('.faq').removeClass('faq_open').hide();


		faq_track(text);//
	

	/* FAQ SEARCH */

	var found_keywords = [];
	var found_counter = 0;
	faq_keywords.forEach(function (k) {

		var count_keyword = 0;

		var k_array = k.split(" ");
		for (var i=0; i<k_array.length; i++) {
			var k1 = k_array[i];
			if (text.match(k1)) count_keyword++;
		}
 
		if (k_array.length == count_keyword) {
			found_counter++;
			found_keywords[found_counter] = k;
		}
	});

	for (const k1 in found_keywords) {
		var keyword1 = found_keywords[k1];

		for (const k2 in found_keywords) {
			var keyword2 = found_keywords[k2];
			if (keyword1 != keyword2 && keyword2.match(keyword1)) delete found_keywords[k1];
		}
	}


	if (found_keywords.length > 0) {	

		var keywords = '';
		var divider = '';
		for (const k in found_keywords) {
			keywords += divider + found_keywords[k];
			divider = ',';
		}

		$('.faq').removeClass('faq_open');

		faq_article = '';
		var divider = '';
		$('.faq_articles .faq').each(function() {
			var faqid = $(this).attr('id').replace('faq_', '');
			faq_article += divider + faqid;
			divider = ',';
		});

		var data = new Object();
		data['keywords'] = keywords;
		data['faq_article'] = faq_article;
		$.post(server_url + "/faq.html?a=search", data, function(faq_result) {

			var faq_results = [];
			var first_faqid = 0;
			var first_position = 999;

			for (const i in faq_result) {

				var faqid = faq_result[i]['id'];
				var position = faq_result[i]['pos'];
				if (position < first_position) {
					first_faqid = faqid;
					first_position = position;
				}

				if ($('#faq_' + faqid).length == 0) {
					
					/* new article */

					var title = faq_result[i]['title'];
					var content = faq_result[i]['content'];
					var link = String(faq_result[i]['link']);
					var link_text = faq_result[i]['link_text'];

					if (link.match(/open/) && (order != 'plan_public' || $('body').hasClass('framework_guest'))) {
						link = '';
					}

					if (content == '') {

						/* link */
						var faq_new = $('#faq_0').clone();
						faq_new.attr('id', 'faq_' + faqid);
						faq_new.find('.faq_title').html(title);

						if (link.match(/open/)) {
							faq_new.attr('href', 'javascript:void(0);').attr('onclick', link);
							title = title.replace(/->/g, '<span class="icon icon-arrow-right2" style="font-size: 70%;"></span>');
							faq_new.find('.faq_title').html(title);
						} else {
							if (link.match(/http/)) {
								faq_new.attr('href', link);
							} else  {
								var href = server_url + link;
								if (link.match("mailto:")) href = link;
								faq_new.attr('href', href);
							}
						}

						$('#faq_0').after(faq_new);

					} else {

						/* article */
						var faq_new = $('#faq_1').clone();
						faq_new.attr('id', 'faq_' + faqid).removeClass('tr_cancellation');
						faq_new.find('.faq_title').html(title);		
						faq_new.find('.faq_content').html(content);

						if (link != '') {
							if (link.match(/http/)) {
								if (link_text != '') faq_new.find('.faq_link').html('<a class="form_button_2 form_button_2_highlight" href="' + link + '">' + link_text + '</a>');
								else faq_new.find('.faq_link').remove();
							}
							else if (link.match(/open/)) {
								if (link_text != '') faq_new.find('.faq_link').html('<a class="form_button_2 form_button_2_highlight" href="javascript:void(0);" onclick="' + link + '">' + link_text + '</a>');
								else faq_new.find('.faq_link').remove();
							} else {
								var href = server_url + link;
								if (link.match("mailto:")) href = link;
								if (link_text != '') faq_new.find('.faq_link').html('<a class="form_button_2 form_button_2_highlight" href="' + href + '">' + link_text + '</a>');
								else faq_new.find('.faq_link').remove();
							}
						}
						else faq_new.find('.faq_link').remove();

						$('#faq_1').after(faq_new);
					}
				}

				$('#faq_' + faqid).show();
				faq_results.push(faqid);

				if (faqid == 32) {
					if ($('body').hasClass('framework_guest')) {
						faq_results.push(0);
						show_plan_search();
					}
				}
			}

			if (first_faqid > 0) {
				
				$('.faq_results').addClass('faq_results_found');
				if ($('#faq_' + first_faqid).attr('href')) {
					// link
					$('.faq_links').prepend($('#faq_' + first_faqid));

				} else {
					// article
					$('.faq_articles').prepend($('#faq_' + first_faqid));
				}

			} else {
				$('.faq_results').removeClass('faq_results_found');
			}

			$('#faq_links .faq, #faq_results .faq').each(function() {
				var faqid = parseInt($(this).attr('id').replace("faq_", ''));
				if (!faq_results.includes( faqid )) $(this).hide();
			});


			for (var i=faq_results.length-1; i>=0; i--) {
				if (!$('#faq_' + faq_results[i]).hasClass('faq_link')) {
					$('#faq_1').after($('#faq_' + faq_results[i]));
				}
			}

			refresh_count_results();

		}, 'json');

	} else {

		$('.faq').removeClass('faq_open').hide();

		if (text.length >= 3) {
			if ($('body').hasClass('framework_guest')) {
				$('.faq_resultcount').addClass('faq_resultcount_show').html('1 Ergebnis gefunden');
				show_plan_search();
			} else {
				$('.faq_resultcount').addClass('faq_resultcount_show').html('0 Ergebnisse gefunden');
			}
		} else {
			$('.faq_resultcount').removeClass('faq_resultcount_show').html('');
		}
	}

	var text_array = text.split(' ');

	/* plan menu */
	if (order == 'plan_public') {

		/* REITER */
		$('.submenu_inner a .menu_entry').each(function() {

			var menu_title = $(this).text();
			var menu_action = $(this).parent().attr('data_action');

			for (const i in text_array) {

				var keyword = text_array[i];
				if (keyword != '' && keyword.length >= 3) {

					if (menu_title.toLowerCase().match(keyword)) {

						if ($('#faq_menu_' + menu_action).length == 0) {

							var href = $(this).parent().attr('href');
							var faq_new = $('#faq_0').clone();
							faq_new.attr('id', 'faq_menu_' + menu_action);
							faq_new.find('.faq_title').html('Spielplan <span class="icon icon-arrow-right2" style="font-size: 70%;"></span>' + menu_title);
							faq_new.find('.faq_button').removeClass('icon-arrow-right2').addClass('icon-trophy');
							faq_new.attr('href', href);

							$('#faq_0').before(faq_new);
						}

						$('#faq_menu_' + menu_action).show();
					}
				}
			}
		});

		/* MENÜ: EINSTELLUNGEN */
		$('.public_settings_inner a .public_settings_menu_title').each(function() {

			var menu_title = $(this).text();
			var menu_settings = $(this).parent().attr('data-settings');

			for (const i in text_array) {

				var keyword = text_array[i];
				if (keyword != '' && keyword.length >= 3) {

					if (menu_title.toLowerCase().match(keyword)) {

						if ($('#faq_menu_' + menu_settings).length == 0) {

							var menu_onclick = $(this).parent().attr('onclick');
							var faq_new = $('#faq_0').clone();
							faq_new.attr('id', 'faq_menu_' + menu_settings);
							faq_new.find('.faq_title').html('Einstellungen <span class="icon icon-arrow-right2" style="font-size: 70%;"></span>' + menu_title);
							faq_new.find('.faq_button').removeClass('icon-arrow-right2').addClass('icon-cog');
							faq_new.attr('onclick', menu_onclick);
							faq_new.attr('href', 'javascript:void(0);');

							$('#faq_0').before(faq_new);
						}

						$('#faq_menu_' + menu_settings).show();
					}
				}
			}
		});

		/* BUTTON: EINSTELLUNGEN */
		$('.public_settings_button span:not(.icon)').each(function() {

			var menu_title = $(this).text();

			var found_keywords = 0;
			var count_keywords = 0;
			for (const i in text_array) {

				var keyword = text_array[i];
				if (keyword != '' && keyword.length >= 3) {
					count_keywords++;
					if (menu_title.toLowerCase().match(keyword)) found_keywords++;
				}
			}

			if (found_keywords > 0 && count_keywords == found_keywords) {

				if ($('#faq_menu_settings').length == 0) {

					var menu_onclick = $(this).parent().attr('onclick');
					var faq_new = $('#faq_0').clone();
					faq_new.attr('id', 'faq_menu_settings');
					faq_new.find('.faq_title').html(menu_title);
					faq_new.find('.faq_button').removeClass('icon-arrow-right2').addClass('icon-cog');
					faq_new.attr('onclick', menu_onclick + '; header_faq_close()');
					faq_new.attr('href', 'javascript:void(0);');

					$('#faq_0').after(faq_new);
				}

				$('#faq_menu_settings').show();
			}

		});

		var count_links = 0;
		$('.faq_links .faq').each(function() {
			if ($(this).css('display') == 'block') count_links++;
		});
		if (count_links >= 2) $('#faq_0').hide();

		refresh_count_results();
	}
}

function faq_track(text) {

	var data = new Object();
	data['text'] = text;
	$.post(server_url + "/faq.html?a=track", data);
}

function refresh_count_results() {

	$('.faq_results').addClass('faq_results_found');

	var count = count_results();
	if (count == 1) {
		$('.faq_resultcount').addClass('faq_resultcount_show').html(count + ' Ergebnis gefunden');
	} else {
		$('.faq_resultcount').addClass('faq_resultcount_show').html(count + ' Ergebnisse gefunden');
	}

	resize_faq();
}

function show_plan_search() {

	$('.faq_results').addClass('faq_results_found');

	var search = $('#faq_search').val().toLowerCase();
	search = search.replace(/\./g, '-');
	search = search.replace(/\s+/g, '-');
	search = search.replace(/\-+/g, '-');
	search = search.replace(/ß/g, 'ss');
	search = search.replace(/[^äöüa-z0-9\-]/g, '');
	search = search.replace(/^(\s|\-)*(.+)/, '$2');
	search = search.replace(/(\s*|\-)$/, '');

	$('#faq_' + 0).css('display', 'block').attr('href', server_url + '/suche/' + search);
}

function count_results() {

	var count = 0;
	$('.faq').each(function() {
		if ($(this).css('display') == 'block') count++;
	});
	return count;
}

function faq_open(this_p) {

	var faqid;
	if (typeof(this_p) == 'number') faqid = this_p;
	else if (typeof(this_p) == 'object') faqid = $(this_p).parent().attr('id').replace('faq_', '');

	faq_click(this_p);

	if (!$('#faq_' + faqid).hasClass('faq_open')) {
		$('.faq_open').removeClass('faq_open');
		$('#faq_' + faqid).addClass('faq_open');
	}
}

function faq_click(this_p) {

	var faqid;
	if (typeof(this_p) == 'number') faqid = this_p;
	else if (typeof(this_p) == 'object') faqid = $(this_p).parent().attr('id').replace('faq_', '');

	var data = new Object();
	data['faqid'] = faqid;
	$.post(server_url + '/faq.html?a=click', data);
}

function faq_close(this_p) {

	var faqid = $(this_p).parent().attr('id').replace('faq_', '');

	if ($('#faq_' + faqid).hasClass('faq_open')) {
		$('#faq_' + faqid).removeClass('faq_open');
	}
}

var faq_scroll_start = 0;
function resize_faq() {

	if ($('body').hasClass('header_faq_show')) {

		if ($('.faq_results.faq_results_found').length > 0) {

			var window_height = $(window).height();
			var results_top = $('.faq_results.faq_results_found').position().top;

			$('.faq_results.faq_results_found').css('height', 'auto');
			var results_height = $('.faq_results.faq_results_found').height();
			var results_bottom = results_height + results_top;

			if (results_bottom > window_height-150) {

				$('html,body').addClass('header_faq_bottom');
				$('.faq_results.faq_results_found').css('height', (window_height-results_top) + 'px').css('overflow', 'auto');;
				$('html,body').css('overflow', 'hidden');
			} else {
				$('.header_faq_bottom').removeClass('header_faq_bottom');
				$('html,body').css('overflow', 'auto');
				$('.faq_results.faq_results_found').css('overflow', 'hidden');
			}
		}

		var scroll = $(window).scrollTop();
		var	scroll_range = scroll- faq_scroll_start;
		if (scroll_range < 0) scroll_range *= -1;

		var scroll_diff = 150;
		if ($(window).width() > 800) scroll_diff = 300;
		if (scroll_range > scroll_diff) header_faq_close();

	} else {

		if ($('.header_faq_bottom').length > 0) {
			$('.header_faq_bottom').removeClass('header_faq_bottom');
			$('.faq_results.faq_results_found').css('overflow', 'hidden');
			$('html,body').css('overflow', 'auto');
		}
	}
}

$(document).ready(function() {

	window.addEventListener('scroll', function() { 
		resize_faq();
	});
	window.addEventListener('resize', function() { 
		resize_faq();
	});

	$('#faq_search').on("keydown", (e) => {
		var key = e.charCode ? e.charCode : e.keyCode ? e.keyCode : 0;
		if (key == 27) header_faq_close();	// escape
	});
});
</script>

<script>
var width_backup = $('body').width();
// var public_settings = '';
var public_settings = 1;
var public_settings_reload = false;

function public_settings_toggle(main_open) {

	if ($('body').hasClass('public_settings_show')) {
	// if (public_settings) {
		// close
		public_settings_close(main_open);
	} else {
		public_settings_open();
	}
}

function public_settings_close(main_open) {

	if (typeof(header_faq_close) == 'function') header_faq_close();
	
	
	if (width_backup < 600) {
		// $('.header_package_button').hide();
		// setTimeout("$('.header_package_button').show();", 300);
	}
	public_settings = 0;
	width_backup = $('body').width();
	$('body').removeClass('public_settings_show');

	// $.post(server_url + '/myoverview.html?a=toggle_public_settings&hash=WwkhCP&type=close');
}

function public_settings_open() {
	public_settings = 1;
	width_backup = $('body').width();
	$('body').addClass('public_settings_show');
	// $.post(server_url + '/myoverview.html?a=toggle_public_settings&hash=WwkhCP&type=open');
}

function public_settings_main_open(action) {

	public_settings_close(1);

	document.location.hash = action;
	$('#public_settings a').removeClass('public_settings_selected');

	var action_1 =  action.replace(/[^&]+/, '');
	var action_org = action;
	action = action.replace(/&.+/, '');

	$('#public_settings a[data-settings="' + action + '"]').addClass('public_settings_selected');

	var urls = {
		'fullscreen' 		: server_url + "/fplan.html?a=fullscreen&hash=WwkhCP",
		'planning' 			: server_url + "/fplan.html?a=planning&hash=WwkhCP",
		'edit_prediction' 	: server_url + "/fplan.html?a=edit_prediction&hash=WwkhCP",
		'prediction' 		: server_url + "/fplan.html?a=prediction&hash=WwkhCP",
		'edit_logo' 		: server_url + "/plan/WwkhCP?a=edit_logo",
		'edit_header'		: server_url + "/plan/WwkhCP?a=edit_header",
		'custom_link'		: server_url + "/custom_link.html?hash=WwkhCP" + action_1,
		'plan_todo'			: server_url + "/myoverview.html?a=plan_todo&hash=WwkhCP",
		'integration'		: server_url + "/plan/WwkhCP?a=integration&" + action_1,
		'share'				: server_url + "/plan/WwkhCP?a=share",
	};

	var url = server_url + "/fleague.html?a=" + action_org + "&hash=WwkhCP";
	if (typeof(urls[action]) != "undefined") url = urls[action];
	if (openSettings_anchor != '') {
		url += '#' + openSettings_anchor;
		openSettings_anchor = false;
	}

	public_settings_main_url(url, action);

	$('#public_settings_style').remove();
	$('#public_settings_main').show();
}

function public_settings_main_url(url, action) {

	$('#public_settings_main').html('<div class="public_settigns_main_iframe"><iframe src="' + url + '"></iframe></div><div class="public_settings_main_header"></div><div class="public_settings_main_plantitle">TU Berlin 1. Volleyball Liga Saison WiSe 2025/2026</div><div class="public_settings_main_subtitle">' + $('#public_settings a[data-settings="' + action + '"] .public_settings_menu_title').text() + '</div><div onclick="public_settings_main_back();" class="public_settings_main_back icon icon-arrow-left2"></div><div onclick="public_settings_main_close(0, 1);" class="public_settings_main_close icon icon-cross"></div>');	

	$('body').addClass('public_settings_main_show_overflow');
	setTimeout("$('body').addClass('public_settings_main_show');", 500);

	parent.tb_remove(1);
}

function public_settings_main_back() {
	public_settings_main_close(false, false, true);
	public_settings_open();
}

function public_settings_main_close(reload, close, back) {

	$('#public_settings a').removeClass('public_settings_selected');

	if (back) {
		$('#public_settings_main').hide();
		$('#public_settings_main').html('').css('display', '');
		$('body').removeClass('public_settings_main_show');
		$('body').removeClass('public_settings_main_show_overflow');
	} else {
		$('body').removeClass('public_settings_main_show_overflow');
		$('#public_settings_main').fadeOut(function() {
			$('#public_settings_main').html('').css('display', '');
			$('body').removeClass('public_settings_main_show');
		});
	}

	document.location.hash='';

	if (reload || public_settings_reload) {
		ajax_content();
	}
	if (close) public_settings_close();

	if (typeof(plan_updated) != "undefined" && plan_updated == true) {
		plan_updated = false;
		if (close) showLoading(0);
		ajax_content();
	}
}

function public_settings_main_reload(url) {
	
	if (!($('#public_settings_main iframe').length > 0)) return false;
	if (typeof(url) != "undefined" && url != '') {
		// url
		$('#public_settings_main iframe').attr('src', url);
	} else {
		// default
		$('#public_settings_main iframe').attr('src', $('#public_settings_main iframe').attr('src'));
	}
}

$(document).ready(function() {

	
});

<script>
var lastupdate_interval = false;
var lastupdate_time = 0;
var difftime = 0;
// var timezone_offset_diff = '';
// timezone_offset_diff = 0;
var public_timezone = 'Europe/Berlin';

function get_timestamp() {
	var to = 0;
	// if (timezone_offset_diff > 0) to = (3600*parseInt(timezone_offset_diff));
	return parseInt((Date.now()/1000)+to);
}
function lastupdate_init() {

	if ($('.lastupdate').length == 0) return false;

	difftime = current_timestamp - get_timestamp();
	lastupdate_time = public_timestamp + difftime;
	if (!lastupdate_interval) lastupdate_interval = setInterval("lastupdate_refresh()", 200);
}
function lastupdate_refresh() {

	lastupdate_diff = (get_timestamp() + difftime) - lastupdate_time;

	lastupdate_text = '';
	if (lastupdate_diff >= 60*60*12) {

		/* AFTER 12 hours */

		lastupdate_text = Math.floor(lastupdate_diff / 60 / 60) + ' Stunden';
		var d = new Date(public_timestamp*1000);
		var options_1 = { weekday:'long', day: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year:'numeric', timeZone: public_timezone};
		var options_2 = { hour:'numeric', minute:'numeric', timeZone: public_timezone};
		lastupdate_text = 'Aktualisiert am ' + d.toLocaleDateString('de-DE', options_1) + ' <span class="nowrap">um ' + d.toLocaleTimeString('de-DE', options_2) + ' Uhr</span>';

		$('.lastupdate').html(lastupdate_text);
		clearInterval(lastupdate_interval);
		lastupdate_interval = false;

	} else {

		/* BEFORE 12 hours */

		if (lastupdate_diff >= 60*60*2) lastupdate_text = Math.floor(lastupdate_diff / 60 / 60) + ' Stunden';
		else if (lastupdate_diff >= 60*60) lastupdate_text = '1 Stunde';
		else if (lastupdate_diff >= 120) lastupdate_text = Math.floor(lastupdate_diff / 60) + ' Minuten';
		else if (lastupdate_diff >= 60) lastupdate_text = '1 Minute';
		else if (lastupdate_diff < 60) lastupdate_text = lastupdate_diff + ' Sekunden';
		else if (lastupdate_diff == 1) lastupdate_text = lastupdate_diff + ' Sekunde';
		else lastupdate_text = ' gerade eben';

		var d = new Date(public_timestamp*1000);
		var options = { hour: 'numeric', minute: 'numeric', timeZone: public_timezone};
		lastupdate_text += ' <span class="nowrap">um ' + ' ' + d.toLocaleTimeString('de-DE', options) + ' Uhr</span>';
		lastupdate_text = 'Aktualisiert vor ' + lastupdate_text;

		if ($('.lastupdate').html() != lastupdate_text) $('.lastupdate').html(lastupdate_text);
	}
}

$(document).ready(function(){
	lastupdate_init();
});
</script>

	<script>
	var count_updates = 0;
	function set_public_refresh(data) {

		if (typeof(get_public_visits) == 'function') get_public_visits(data);

		current_timestamp = data.ct;
		if (typeof(lastupdate_init) == "function") lastupdate_init();

		var timestamp = data.pt;
		if (timestamp > public_timestamp) {

			count_updates = 0;
			clearInterval(public_refresh_interval);
			public_refresh_interval = setInterval("get_public_refresh()", public_refresh_time);

			if ($('#content_popup').length == 0) {
				
				/* global */

				if ($('#TB_window').length == 0) $('body').addClass('public_liveupdate_loading');
				ajax_content(false, false, false, true);

			} else {

				/* framework_popup */

				$('body').addClass('public_liveupdate_loading');
				
				var guest = '';
				if (typeof(guest) != "undefined" && guest == 'guest') guest = 'yes';
				data_input = new Object;
				data_input['guest'] = guest;

				$.post(window.location.href, function(data2) {

					$('#content_popup').html(data2.content);

					checkToolTip();
					inits();

					if (typeof(fullscreen_settings_init) == 'function') {
						fullscreen_settings_init();
					}

					$('body').addClass('public_liveupdate_loaded');
					$('body').removeClass('public_liveupdate_loading');

					setTimeout("$('body').removeClass('public_liveupdate_loaded');", 1000);

					var public_box = data2.public_box;
					if (public_box != '') {
						setHeight();
					}

				}, 'json');
			}

			public_timestamp = timestamp;
			if (typeof(lastupdate_init) == "function") lastupdate_init();
		}
	}
	</script>
	


	<script>

/*** SHORT POLLING ***/

var public_timestamp = 1767637874;
var current_timestamp = 1768600657;
var public_refresh_active = false;
var public_refresh_interval = false;
var public_refresh_time = 10000; // ...

var refresh_step_0 = 7000;
var refresh_step_1 = 20000;
var refresh_step_2 = 40000;



function get_public_refresh() {

	if (action == 'print') return false;
	if ($('body').hasClass('public_settings_main_show_overflow')) return false;
	if (ajax_content_progress) return false;
	if (public_refresh_active) return false;
	public_refresh_active = true;

	count_updates++;
	
	if (count_updates == 18) {
		clearInterval(public_refresh_interval);
		public_refresh_interval = setInterval("get_public_refresh()", refresh_step_2);	// ab 5 min
	}
	else if (count_updates == 6) {
		clearInterval(public_refresh_interval);
		public_refresh_interval = setInterval("get_public_refresh()", refresh_step_1);	// ab 1 min
	}
	
	var visit = '';
	if (typeof(visit_hash) != "undefined") visit = "&visit_hash=" + visit_hash;
	$.get(server_url + "/plan/WwkhCP?a=refresh&timestamp=" + public_timestamp + visit + '', function(data) {
		if (data != "") {
			set_public_refresh(data);
		}

		public_refresh_active = false;

	}, 'json');
}

$(document).ready(function(){
	if (!public_refresh_interval) {
		setTimeout('public_refresh_interval = setInterval("get_public_refresh()", public_refresh_time);', refresh_step_0);
	}
});
</script>

			var hash = hash_org.replace(/&.+/, '');
			if (hash == 'integration' || hash == 'print') {
				document.location.hash = '';

		function openSettings(action, geturl) {

		var settings_action = action.replace(/&.+$/, '');
		// console.log(settings_action);
		window.location.href = server_url + '/plan/WwkhCP?a=' + action;
	}

		openSettings('share');
	}
	function plan_print() {
		
		var url = server_url + '/plan/WwkhCP?a=print';

		var parameters = '';
		if (action != '') parameters = '#set=' + action;

		
		window.location.href = url + parameters;
	}
	function plan_integration(box, customid) {

		if (typeof(box) != "undefined") {
			box = '&box=' + box;
		} else box = '';

		if (typeof(customid) != "undefined") {
			customid = '&id=' + customid;
		} else customid = '';

		openSettings('integration' + box + customid);
		// window.location.href = server_url + '/plan/WwkhCP#integration' + box;
	}

	function change_editMatch(matchid) {
		tb_remove();
		setTimeout("editMatch(" + matchid + ");", 500);
	}

		tb_remove();

	function showMatch(this_p, matchid) {
		
		if (ajax_content_progress) return false;

		if (this_p != '' && $(this_p).html().match(/editMatch/g)) return false;
		
		if (matchid > 0) {
			var strURL = server_url + "/plan/WwkhCP?a=show_match&matchid=" + matchid + public_guest_parameter;
			tb_show('', strURL + "&KeepThis=true&TB_iframe=true&height=400&width=862&modal=true", '', this_p);
		}
	}
	function showTournamentMatch(this_p, matchid, round) {
		
		if (ajax_content_progress) return false;

		if (this_p != '' && $(this_p).html().match(/editMatch/g)) return false;
		
		if (matchid > 0 && round > 0) {
			var strURL = server_url + "/plan/WwkhCP?a=show_match&matchid=" + matchid + "&round=" + round;
			tb_show('', strURL + "&KeepThis=true&TB_iframe=true&height=400&width=862&modal=true", '', this_p);	
		}
	}
	function showCrossMatch(this_p, matchid, teamrank) {
		
		if (ajax_content_progress) return false;
		
		if (this_p != '' && $(this_p).html().match(/editMatch/g)) return false;
		
		if (matchid > 0) {
			var strURL = server_url + "/plan/WwkhCP?a=show_match&matchid=" + matchid + '&teamrank=' + teamrank + public_guest_parameter;
			tb_show('', strURL + "&KeepThis=true&TB_iframe=true&height=400&width=862&modal=true", '', this_p);
		}
	}

	<style>

	<script>
	var visits_online = 0;
	var visits_today = 0;
	var visits_yesterday = 0;
	var visits_sum = 0;
	var visits_change = 0;
	var visit_hash = '8eb0f7caf2938e4c2985131a898d4a5c';

	function get_public_visits(data) {
		
		if (visits_change == 0) {
			if (typeof(data.hash) != "undefined") {

				visit_hash = data.hash;
				if (visit_hash != '') {

					visits_online = data.online;
					visits_today = data.today;
					visits_yesterday = data.yesterday;
					visits_sum = data.sum;
					
					visits_change = 2;
					set_public_visit('.visits_online', visits_online, 'first');
					set_public_visit('.visits_today', visits_today, 'first');
					// set_public_visit('.visits_yesterday', visits_yesterday, 'first');
					// set_public_visit('.visits_sum', visits_sum, 'first');
					
					return true;

				} else return false;
			}
		}
	}

	function set_public_visit(name, count, action) {
		
		var count_old = parseInt($(name + ' b').html());
		if (count_old != count) {
			
			if (count > count_old) count_old++;
			else if (count < count_old) count_old--;
			
			$(name).addClass('change');
			
			var timeout = 20;
			if (action == 'first') {
				timeout = 500;
			} else {
				$(name + ' b').html(count_old);
			}
			setTimeout("set_public_visit('" + name + "', '" + count + "')", timeout);
		} else {
			$(name).removeClass('change');
			visits_change--;
		}
	}
	</script>

<script>
	function ga_init() {

		if (document.cookie.indexOf('ga-disable') > -1 || navigator.doNotTrack === '1') {

			/* NOT ACCEPT */

			window['ga-disable'] = true; 

			

			if (typeof(google_conversion) == 'function') google_conversion();
			if (typeof(digistore24_orderform) == 'function') digistore24_orderform();
			if (typeof(spreadshirt_shopclient) == 'function') spreadshirt_shopclient();
		} 
		else {

			/* ACCEPT */

						
				(function(i,s,o,g,r,a,m){i['GoogleAnalyticsObject']=r;i[r]=i[r]||function(){
				(i[r].q=i[r].q||[]).push(arguments)},i[r].l=1*new Date();a=s.createElement(o),
				m=s.getElementsByTagName(o)[0];a.async=1;a.src=g;m.parentNode.insertBefore(a,m)
				})(window,document,'script','//www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=G-YG42SVD68T','ga');
				
				window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
				function gtag(){dataLayer.push(arguments);}

				gtag('js', new Date());
				gtag('config', 'G-YG42SVD68T'); /* global */
				gtag('config', 'G-K04ZZJEQYP'); /* meinspielplan */

				function globalAnalytics(page) {
					if (page != '') {
						gtag('event', 'page_view', { 'send_to': 'G-YG42SVD68T', 'page_path': page });
					} else {
						gtag('event', 'page_view', { 'send_to': 'G-YG42SVD68T' });
					}
				}

				function localAnalytics(page) {
					gtag('set', {'visitor': 'guest', 'send_to': 'G-K04ZZJEQYP' });
					if (page != '') {
						gtag('event', 'page_view', { 'send_to': 'G-K04ZZJEQYP', 'page_path': page });
					} else {
						gtag('event', 'page_view', { 'send_to': 'G-K04ZZJEQYP' });
					}
				}
			
				
			
			

			if (typeof(google_conversion) == 'function') google_conversion();
			if (typeof(google_recaptcha) == 'function') google_recaptcha();
			if (typeof(digistore24_orderform) == 'function') digistore24_orderform();
			if (typeof(spreadshirt_shopclient) == 'function') spreadshirt_shopclient();
		}
	}
</script>




<script type="text/javascript">
	function updateAnalytics(page) {
				if (typeof(globalAnalytics) == 'function') {
			globalAnalytics(page);
			page = page.replace(server_url, "");
			localAnalytics(page);
		}
			}	
</script>

<script>
function shariff_init() {

	var buttonsContainer = $('.shariff');
	new Shariff(buttonsContainer);

	$(document).ready(function() {
		setTimeout("$('.shariff a').addClass('tooltip'); checkToolTip(); shariff_overwrite();", 200);
	});
}
function shariff_overwrite() {
	$('.shariff-button a').each(function() {
		$(this).attr('href', $(this).attr('href').replace(/%23.+/, ''));
		$(this).attr('href', $(this).attr('href').replace(/%3F.+/, ''));
	});
}

shariff_init();
</script>
